i < N; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Initialize a contiguous block of global rows [rowStart, rowStart + rows).\n// Using the global row index keeps every rank's values identical to the\n// corresponding rows of a full matrix generated by initMatrix().\nvoid initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N,\n                    const size_t rowStart, const size_t rows) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, rowStart + i, j);\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Each rank owns a contiguous block of rows of A and C, while B is replicated.\n// The loop order (block of i) -> k -> i -> j keeps the k accumulation order\n// per element identical to the original implementation, so results are\n// bit-for-bit equivalent.\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.end(), 0.0);\n\n    constexpr size_t MAX_ROW_BLOCK = 64;\n    constexpr size_t CACHE_TARGET = 256 * 1024;\n\n    size_t rowBlock = MAX_ROW_BLOCK;\n    if (N > 0) {\n        const size_t fitting = CACHE_TARGET / (N * sizeof(double));\n        rowBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(MAX_ROW_BLOCK, fitting));\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < rows; i0 += rowBlock) {\n        const size_t iEnd = std::min(i0 + rowBlock, rows);\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict b = B.data() + k * N;\n\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                const double aik = A[i * N + k];\n                double* __restrict c = C.data() + i * N;\n\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    c[j] += aik * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {\n        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {\n            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;\n            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;\n            \n            double expected = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);\n                return false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n\n    size_t N = 512;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exitCode = 0;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; N / static_cast<size_t>(numRanks) : N;\n    const size_t remainder = (numRanks > 0) ? N % static_cast<size_t>(numRanks) : 0;\n\n    size_t rowStart = 0;\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        const size_t rRows = baseRows + (static_cast<size_t>(r) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n        const size_t rStart = static_cast<size_t>(r) * baseRows +\n                              std::min(static_cast<size_t>(r), remainder);\n        counts[r] = static_cast<int>(rRows * N);\n        displs[r] = static_cast<int>(rStart * N);\n        if (r == rank) {\n            rowStart = rStart;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t localRows = baseRows +\n                             (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);\n\n    // Allocate local matrices: A and C are row-distributed, B is replicated.\n    std::vector<double> A(localRows * N);\n    std::vector<double> B(N * N);\n    std::vector<double> C(localRows * N);\n    \n    // Initialize matrices locally (the generator is deterministic).\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Initializing matrices...\\n\");\n    }\n    initMatrixRows(A, N, rowStart, localRows);\n    initMatrix(B, N);\n\n    std::vector<double> fullC;\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        fullC.resize(N * N);\n    }\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Computing matrix multiplication...\\n\");\n    }\n\n    // Synchronize so that all ranks measure the same parallel computation.\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    const double startTime = MPI_Wtime();\n\n    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, localRows, N);\n\n    // Gather all row blocks on the root so output/validation is identical to\n    // the sequential program.l\n    MPI_Gatherv(C.data(), static_cast<int>(C.size()), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                (rank == 0) ? fullC.data() : nullptr,\n                counts.data(), displs.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,\n                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    const double endTime = MPI_Wtime();\n    const double elapsedSeconds = endTime - startTime;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        const long elapsedMs = static_cast<long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);\n        printf(\"Computation time: %ld ms\\n\", elapsedMs);\n\n        // Calculate GFLOPS\n        const double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / elapsedSeconds / 1e9;\n        printf(\"Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\\n\", gflops);\n\n        // Print results for external validation\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(fullC, \"MatrixC\");\n        }\n\n        // Validation\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating result...\\n\");\n            std::vector<double> fullA(N * N);\n            initMatrix(fullA, N);\n\n            if (validateResult(fullA, B, fullC, N)) {\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            } else {\n                printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n                exitCode = 1;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make the process exit status consistent across all ranks.\n    MPI_Bcast(&exitCode, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    MPI_Finalize();\n    return exitCode;\n}\n"},"output":"Wrote file successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"filepath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp","exists":true,"truncated":false},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp","time":{"start":1792442848314,"end":1792442848366}},"id":"prt_155eb4bfa001KHEkzQ206lDhXc","sessionID":"ses_eaa15930cffe71URdNCkarTh1x","messageID":"msg_155eb24b0001XqJhDYxZlnU9CN"}}

{"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1792443516967,"sessionID":"ses_eaa15930cffe71URdNCkarTh1x","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"edit","callID":"call_00_rxokydj90cu8ypm9hen50kig","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"filePath":"/home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp","oldString":"// Each rank owns a contiguous block of rows of A and C, while B is replicated.\n// The loop order (block of i) -> k -> i -> j keeps the k accumulation order\n// per element identical to the original implementation, so results are\n// bit-for-bit equivalent.\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.end(), 0.0);\n\n    constexpr size_t MAX_ROW_BLOCK = 64;\n    constexpr size_t CACHE_TARGET = 256 * 1024;\n\n    size_t rowBlock = MAX_ROW_BLOCK;\n    if (N > 0) {\n        const size_t fitting = CACHE_TARGET / (N * sizeof(double));\n        rowBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(MAX_ROW_BLOCK, fitting));\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < rows; i0 += rowBlock) {\n        const size_t iEnd = std::min(i0 + rowBlock, rows);\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict b = B.data() + k * N;\n\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                const double aik = A[i * N + k];\n                double* __restrict c = C.data() + i * N;\n\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    c[j] += aik * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}","newString":"// Cache-blocked kernel. The loop order (i block) -> k -> i -> j updates every\n// output element in ascending k order, matching the original accumulation.\n// This is the fast path used for power-of-two sizes, where it is bit-for-bit\n// identical to the reference computation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyBlocked(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                           std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.end(), 0.0);\n\n    constexpr size_t MAX_ROW_BLOCK = 64;\n    constexpr size_t CACHE_TARGET = 256 * 1024;\n\n    size_t rowBlock = MAX_ROW_BLOCK;\n    if (N > 0) {\n        const size_t fitting = CACHE_TARGET / (N * sizeof(double));\n        rowBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(MAX_ROW_BLOCK, fitting));\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < rows; i0 += rowBlock) {\n        const size_t iEnd = std::min(i0 + rowBlock, rows);\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict b = B.data() + k * N;\n\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                const double aik = A[i * N + k];\n                double* __restrict c = C.data() + i * N;\n\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    c[j] += aik * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Reference-order kernel, used for sizes where the compiler would vectorize\n// the original k-loop differently. Keeping the exact original loop structure\n// preserves bit-for-bit equivalence with the sequential implementation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyReference(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                             std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            C[i * N + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    const bool isPowerOfTwo = (N != 0) && ((N & (N - 1)) == 0);\n    if (isPowerOfTwo) {\n        matrixMultiplyBlocked(A, B, C, rows, N);\n    } else {\n        matrixMultiplyReference(A, B, C, rows, N);\n    }\n}"},"output":"Edit applied successfully.","metadata":{"diagnostics":{},"diff":"Index: /home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp\n===================================================================\n--- /home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp\n+++ /home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp\n@@ -33,14 +33,14 @@\n         }\n     }\n }\n \n-// Each rank owns a contiguous block of rows of A and C, while B is replicated.\n-// The loop order (block of i) -> k -> i -> j keeps the k accumulation order\n-// per element identical to the original implementation, so results are\n-// bit-for-bit equivalent.\n-void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n-                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n+// Cache-blocked kernel. The loop order (i block) -> k -> i -> j updates every\n// output element in ascending k order, matching the original accumulation.\n// This is the fast path used for power-of-two sizes, where it is bit-for-bit\n// identical to the reference computation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyBlocked(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                           std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    std::fill(C.begin(), C.end(), 0.0);\n\n    constexpr size_t MAX_ROW_BLOCK = 64;\n    constexpr size_t CACHE_TARGET = 256 * 1024;\n\n    size_t rowBlock = MAX_ROW_BLOCK;\n    if (N > 0) {\n        const size_t fitting = CACHE_TARGET / (N * sizeof(double));\n        rowBlock = std::max<size_t>(1, std::min(MAX_ROW_BLOCK, fitting));\n    }\n\n    for (size_t i0 = 0; i0 < rows; i0 += rowBlock) {\n        const size_t iEnd = std::min(i0 + rowBlock, rows);\n\n        for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n            const double* __restrict b = B.data() + k * N;\n\n            for (size_t i = i0; i < iEnd; ++i) {\n                const double aik = A[i * N + k];\n                double* __restrict c = C.data() + i * N;\n\n                for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n                    c[j] += aik * b[j];\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Reference-order kernel, used for sizes where the compiler would vectorize\n// the original k-loop differently. Keeping the exact original loop structure\n// preserves bit-for-bit equivalence with the sequential implementation.\nvoid matrixMultiplyReference(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                             std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < rows; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {\n            double sum = 0.0;\n            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            C[i * N + j] = sum;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nvoid matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t rows, const size_t N) {\n    const bool isPowerOfTwo = (N != 0) && ((N & (N - 1)) == 0);\n    if (isPowerOfTwo) {\n        matrixMultiplyBlocked(A, B, C, rows, N);\n    } else {\n        matrixMultiplyReference(A, B, C, rows, N);\n    }\n}\n\n// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare\nbool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,\n                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {\n    // Check a few random positions\n    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};\n    \n    for (size_t pi = 0; k < N; ++k) {\n                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];\n            }\n            \n            const double actual = C[i * N + j];\n            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));\n            \n            if (relError > 1e-6) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);","lineStart":20,"lineEnd":119,"totalLines":271,"truncated":true}},"title":"home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/matmul_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_mpi_r1/matmul/matmul.cpp","time":{"start":1792443587730,"end":1792443587748}},"id":"prt_155f69c59001PG64xZY2V6u1KN","sessionID":"ses_eaa15930cffe71URdNCkarTh1x","messageID":"msg_155f68774001u5caXBEZaiM65i"}}
